const userMessages = document.getElementById('userMessages');
const gptMessages = document.getElementById('gptMessages');
function displayMessage(message, id) {
const messageDiv = document.createElement('div');
messageDiv.innerHTML = `<strong>${id}: </strong>${message}`;
messageDiv.className = 'message';
id === 'user' ? userMessages.appendChild(messageDiv) : gptMessages.appendChild(messageDiv);
}
function handleUserMessage(userMessage) {
// Display user message
displayMessage(userMessage, 'user');
// Simulate GPT response
const gptResponse = "GPT: Hello! How can I assist you today?";
displayMessage(gptResponse, 'gpt');
}

handleUserMessage(userMessage);
